Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the core space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 567 and the convergence rate is O(320/n^2).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by core generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 567, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 320. QED.
